# Cruiser Docks: buildings and rooms

Deck 5 · Cruiser Docks. Every building in the district, floor by floor, with every room and what it does (Jordan, 27 Nov 2026: "I want to know what each room does").

**169 buildings, 56,487 rooms.**

This doc is written from the data by `Invicta/tools/rooms_doc.py`; change the building's room program (`Invicta/tools/room_programs/`) or its plan (`Invicta/data/models/`), then re-run it. Rooms with the same name on a floor are counted together; floors that are exactly alike are shown once. Room plans are **proposed** unless a building says otherwise. Sizes are US first, metric in brackets.

## P1 · Cruiser Gates

The three Cruiser Gates and six cruiser berths, supply quays

### P1-01 · Cruiser Gate 1 Berths

**What it is for:** Two berths behind Cruiser Gate 1 where escort cruisers dock nose first to load stores, take on crew and get repairs.

13 ft (4 m) tall, two open berths; cabins and gangway towers, 8,180 sq ft (760 m²) of rooms. Room plan proposed.

**Look:** A pair of vast floodlit berths with striped edges and rows of mooring arms, the gate's huge doors at one end.

#### Level 1 · Berth floor

Floor at deck level; 13 ft (4 m) floor to floor.

| Room | How many | Floor area | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| Berth control cabin | 2 | 1,430 sq ft (133 m²) each | The berth master watches the cruiser's mooring arms, power and hoses from here. |
| Line handlers' shelter | 2 | 720 sq ft (67 m²) each | Shelter and kit for the crew who make the cruiser fast. |
| Shore connection house | 1 | 2,580 sq ft (240 m²) | Where shore power, water, air and data cables plug into the cruiser. |
| Fuel valve house | 1 | 1,290 sq ft (120 m²) | Valves that open the fuel line from the fuel farm to the cruiser. |
| Berth 1A (open air) | 1 | 9,602,600 sq ft (892,110 m²) | A berth for one 2 km escort cruiser, which docks nose first towards Cruiser Gate 1, held by mooring arms along both sides. Inside: Mooring arms. |
| Service apron (open air) | 1 | 2,398,400 sq ft (222,820 m²) | The strip between the two berths, with shore connections, fuel valves and the gangway towers. Inside: Gangway towers. |
| Berth 1B (open air) | 1 | 9,602,600 sq ft (892,110 m²) | The second berth behind Cruiser Gate 1, for another escort cruiser. Inside: Mooring arms. |

### P1-16 · Gates Service Shaft

**What it is for:** The Cruiser Gates' shaft down to the service layer.

49 ft (15 m) tall, 2 above deck, 1 below, 868,100 sq ft (80,650 m²) of rooms. Room plan proposed.

**Look:** A square tower of ribbed grey panels with a big goods lift door on each side and pipe ducts climbing its walls.

#### Level B1 · Service layer landing

Floor at 20 ft (6 m) below the deck; 20 ft (6 m) floor to floor.

| Room | How many | Floor area | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| Service layer landing | 1 | 209,200 sq ft (19,440 m²) | The foot of the shaft in the service layer, where tunnels lead off to the precinct's pipes, cables and plant. |
| Tunnel portal | 1 | 208,600 sq ft (19,380 m²) | Where the service tunnels meet the shaft. |

#### Level 1 · Lift landing

Floor at deck level; 30 ft (9 m) floor to floor.

| Room | How many | Floor area | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| Shaft and lift landing | 1 | 385,100 sq ft (35,770 m²) | The top of the shaft that drops 197 ft (60 m) to the service layer: goods lifts, a crew lift and a stair land here. Inside: Goods lift cage, Crew lift and stair. |
| Lift control room | 1 | 650 sq ft (60 m²) | Operators run the goods lifts and watch the shaft cameras from here. |
| Loading bay | 1 | 1,690 sq ft (157 m²) | Trucks back in here to load and unload goods going to or from the service layer. |
| Crew check-in | 1 | 430 sq ft (40 m²) | Crews going down sign out, pick up radios and gas detectors and sign back in when they return. |
| Tool store | 1 | 870 sq ft (80 m²) | Tools, harnesses and lamps for work in the service layer. |
| Staff toilets and showers | 1 | 750 sq ft (70 m²) | Toilets, showers and changing space for the staff. |
| Holding store | 18 | 1,570 sq ft (146 m²) each | Short-term storage for goods waiting for the lift. |

#### Level 2 · Winding room

Floor at 30 ft (9 m) above the deck; 20 ft (6 m) floor to floor.

| Room | How many | Floor area | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| Shaft and lift landing | 1 | 385,100 sq ft (35,770 m²) | The top of the shaft that drops 197 ft (60 m) to the service layer: goods lifts, a crew lift and a stair land here. Inside: Goods lift cage, Crew lift and stair. |
| Winding machinery | 1 | 1,610 sq ft (150 m²) | The motors, brakes and cables that drive the shaft lifts. |
| Shaft ventilation fans | 1 | 720 sq ft (67 m²) | Fans that push fresh air down the shaft and the service tunnels. |
| Machine gallery | 19 | 1,590 sq ft (148 m²) each | Walkways around the lift machinery for inspection. |

## P2 · Fleet Stores

Cruiser fuel, munitions, stores and repair parts (with Ferrum)

### P2-01 · Cruiser Munitions Magazine

**What it is for:** The magazine holding shells and missiles for the six escort cruisers.

33 ft (10 m) tall, 1 above deck, 1 below, 8,611,300 sq ft (800,020 m²) of rooms. Room plan proposed.

| Ductwork gallery | 1 | 1,160 sq ft (108 m²) | Where the main air ducts leave the plant and turn toward the streets. |

### P4-02 · Container Yard
